# Prehistorica Complete Verified Database Media Audit Consolidated Report (All 502 Taxa)

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Audit Status**: **PROPOSED ONLY**. Zero database writes, file moves, image replacements, commits, pushes, or deployments executed.
> Every taxon (positions 1 through 502) audited under strict non-fallback Wikimedia Commons API lookups.
